- **Passed:** The case’s check is met and no forbidden answer appears.
- **Hit@1:** The first result is a best answer.
- **Recall@10:** Share of the right answers found in the top 10.
- **MRR:** 1 ÷ the rank of the first best answer, averaged.
- **nDCG@10:** How good the order of the top 10 is, from 0 to 1 (best = 2 points, ok = 1, lower ranks count less).
- **False positives:** Share of noise queries that returned anything.
- **p50 / p95:** The typical and the slow query time.
- **Cost per 1,000 queries:** What 1,000 queries cost in API fees: 0 for an engine that runs locally.
- **Sent to the LLM:** Queries the deterministic search wasn’t confident about (top score under CONFIDENT_SCORE), answered by the LLM instead.
- **Cut off:** LLM answers that hit the output limit: only the keys written in full before the cut are kept.

- **The LLM wins:** lore, web: identify, sentence, noise, effect, units, type, weather, category, appearance, stats, negation
- **The hybrid wins:** web: GO syntax, web: Q&A, injection, family, name
- **Even:** ability name, learnset, move name, number

### Invented keys

Keys the LLM answered that the data doesn’t hold. They are left out of its scored results.

None: every key it answered is in the data.

### Right items missed on exact lists (type, stats, number)

Best items absent from anywhere in the LLM’s answer, over the 32 cases of these categories.

- 71. "speed over 100" (stats): 7 of 17 missing · pidgeot, kadabra, rapidash, gengar, scyther, electabuzz, tauros
